    22→var ErrSessionSkipped = errors.New("session skipped")
    23→
    24→// handleGeminiSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Gemini CLI.
    25→func handleGeminiSessionStart() error {
    26→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
    27→}
    28→
    29→// handleGeminiSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Gemini CLI.
    30→// This fires when the user explicitly exits the session (via "exit" or "logout" commands).
    31→// Note: The primary checkpoint creation happens in AfterAgent (equivalent to Claude's Stop).
    32→// SessionEnd serves as a cleanup/fallback - it will commit any uncommitted changes that
    33→// weren't captured by AfterAgent (e.g., if the user exits mid-response).
    34→func handleGeminiSessionEnd() error {
    35→	// Set up logging context
    36→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    37→	if err != nil {
    38→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    39→	}
    40→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    41→
    42→	// Commit any uncommitted changes (consumes stdin)
    43→	if err := commitWithMetadataGemini(); err != nil {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to commit metadata: %v\n", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Mark session as ended (use persisted session ID since stdin consumed)
    48→	entireSessionID, err := paths.ReadCurrentSession()
    49→	if err != nil {
    50→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read current session: %v\n", err)
    51→	}
    52→	if entireSessionID != "" {
    53→		logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end-mark",
    54→			slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
    55→			slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    56→			slog.String("entire_session_id", entireSessionID),
    57→		)
    58→
    59→		if err := markSessionEnded(entireSessionID); err != nil {
    60→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
    61→		}
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	return nil
    65→}

    80→// parseGeminiSessionEnd parses the session-end hook input and validates transcript.
    81→func parseGeminiSessionEnd() (*geminiSessionContext, error) {
    82→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)

    91→
    92→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    93→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
    94→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
    95→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    96→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),

    99→
   100→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   101→	if sessionID == "" {
   102→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   106→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {

   109→
   110→	return &geminiSessionContext{

   112→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
   113→	}, nil
   114→}

   116→// setupGeminiSessionDir creates session directory and copies transcript.
   117→func setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   118→	ctx.sessionDir = paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(ctx.sessionID)
   119→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(ctx.sessionDir)
   120→	if err != nil {
   121→		sessionDirAbs = ctx.sessionDir
   122→	}
   123→	ctx.sessionDirAbs = sessionDirAbs
   124→
   125→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   130→	if err := copyFile(ctx.transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   131→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   132→	}
   133→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   134→
   135→	transcriptData, err := os.ReadFile(ctx.transcriptPath)
   136→	if err != nil {
   137→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   138→	}
   139→	ctx.transcriptData = transcriptData
   140→
   141→	return nil
   142→}
   143→
   144→// extractGeminiMetadata extracts prompts, summary, and modified files from transcript.
   145→func extractGeminiMetadata(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   146→	allPrompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts(ctx.transcriptData)
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", err)
   149→	}
   150→	ctx.allPrompts = allPrompts
   151→
   152→	promptFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   153→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   154→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   155→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   156→	}
   157→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   158→
   159→	summary, err := geminicli.ExtractLastAssistantMessage(ctx.transcriptData)
   160→	if err != nil {
   161→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", err)
   162→	}
   163→	ctx.summary = summary
   164→
   165→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   166→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   167→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   168→	}
   169→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   170→
   171→	modifiedFiles, err := geminicli.ExtractModifiedFiles(ctx.transcriptData)
   172→	if err != nil {
   173→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", err)
   174→	}
   175→	ctx.modifiedFiles = modifiedFiles
   176→
   177→	lastPrompt := ""
   178→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   179→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   180→	}
   181→	ctx.commitMessage = generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   182→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", ctx.commitMessage)
   183→
   184→	return nil
   185→}

   187→// commitGeminiSession commits the session changes using the strategy.
   188→func commitGeminiSession(ctx *geminiSessionContext) error {
   189→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   190→	if err != nil {
   191→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID)
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   197→	}
   198→	if preState != nil {
   199→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Loaded pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files, start message index: %d\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles), preState.StartMessageIndex)
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state
   203→	var startMessageIndex int
   204→	if preState != nil {
   205→		startMessageIndex = preState.StartMessageIndex
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Calculate token usage for this prompt/response cycle (Gemini-specific)
   209→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   210→	if ctx.transcriptPath != "" {
   211→		usage, tokenErr := geminicli.CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(ctx.transcriptPath, startMessageIndex)
   212→		if tokenErr != nil {
   213→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   214→		} else if usage != nil && usage.APICallCount > 0 {
   215→			tokenUsage = usage
   216→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Token usage for this checkpoint: input=%d, output=%d, cache_read=%d, api_calls=%d\n",
   217→				tokenUsage.InputTokens, tokenUsage.OutputTokens, tokenUsage.CacheReadTokens, tokenUsage.APICallCount)
   218→		}
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   222→	newFiles, deletedFiles, err := ComputeFileChanges(preState)
   223→	if err != nil {
   224→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(ctx.modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   228→	relNewFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(newFiles, repoRoot)
   229→	relDeletedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(deletedFiles, repoRoot)
   230→
   231→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   232→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   233→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   234→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   235→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   236→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   237→		}
   238→		return nil
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   242→
   243→	contextFile := filepath.Join(ctx.sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   244→	if err := createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, ctx.commitMessage, ctx.sessionID, ctx.allPrompts, ctx.summary); err != nil {
   245→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", ctx.sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   248→
   249→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	strat := GetStrategy()
   255→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   256→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	// Get agent type from the hook agent (determined by which hook command is running)
   260→	// This is authoritative - if we're in "entire hooks gemini session-end", it's Gemini CLI
   261→	hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   262→	if agentErr != nil {
   263→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", agentErr)
   264→	}
   265→	agentType := hookAgent.Type()
   266→
   267→	// Get transcript identifier at start from pre-prompt state
   268→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   269→	if preState != nil {
   270→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   271→	}
   272→
   273→	saveCtx := strategy.SaveContext{
   274→		SessionID:                   ctx.sessionID,
   275→		ModifiedFiles:               relModifiedFiles,
   276→		NewFiles:                    relNewFiles,
   277→		DeletedFiles:                relDeletedFiles,
   278→		MetadataDir:                 ctx.sessionDir,
   279→		MetadataDirAbs:              ctx.sessionDirAbs,
   280→		CommitMessage:               ctx.commitMessage,
   281→		TranscriptPath:              ctx.transcriptPath,
   282→		AuthorName:                  author.Name,
   283→		AuthorEmail:                 author.Email,
   284→		AgentType:                   agentType,
   285→		TranscriptLinesAtStart:      startMessageIndex,
   286→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   287→		TokenUsage:                  tokenUsage,
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(saveCtx); err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(ctx.sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   295→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session saved successfully\n")
   299→	return nil
   300→}
   301→
   302→// logFileChanges logs the modified, new, and deleted files to stderr.
   303→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   304→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   305→	for _, file := range modified {
   306→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   307→	}
   308→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   309→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   310→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   311→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   312→		}
   313→	}
   314→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   315→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   316→		for _, file := range deleted {
   317→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   318→		}
   319→	}
   320→}
   321→
   322→// commitWithMetadataGemini commits the Gemini session changes with metadata.
   323→// This is a Gemini-specific version that parses JSON transcripts correctly.
   324→func commitWithMetadataGemini() error {
   325→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
   326→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
   327→		return nil
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	ctx, err := parseGeminiSessionEnd()
   331→	if err != nil {
   332→		if errors.Is(err, ErrSessionSkipped) {
   333→			return nil // Skip signaled
   334→		}
   335→		return err
   336→	}
   337→
   338→	if err := setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx); err != nil {
   339→		return err
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	if err := extractGeminiMetadata(ctx); err != nil {
   343→		return err
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	return commitGeminiSession(ctx)
   347→}
   348→
   349→// createContextFileForGemini creates a context.md file for Gemini sessions.
   350→func createContextFileForGemini(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   351→	var sb strings.Builder
   352→
   353→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   354→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Session ID: %s\n", sessionID))
   355→	sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage))
   356→
   357→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   358→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   359→		for i, p := range prompts {
   360→			sb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p))
   361→		}
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	if summary != "" {
   365→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   366→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   367→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   371→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   372→	}
   373→	return nil
   374→}
   375→
   376→// handleGeminiBeforeTool handles the BeforeTool hook for Gemini CLI.
   377→// This is similar to Claude Code's PreToolUse hook but applies to all tools.
   378→func handleGeminiBeforeTool() error {
   379→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   380→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()

   389→	}

   391→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   392→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-before-tool",
   393→		slog.String("hook", "before-tool"),
   394→		slog.String("hook_type", "tool"),
   395→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   396→		slog.String("tool_name", input.ToolName),
   397→	)
   398→
   399→	// For now, BeforeTool is mainly for logging and potential future use
   400→	// We don't need to do anything special before tool execution
   401→	return nil
   402→}
   403→
   404→// handleGeminiAfterTool handles the AfterTool hook for Gemini CLI.
   405→// This is similar to Claude Code's PostToolUse hook but applies to all tools.
   406→func handleGeminiAfterTool() error {
   407→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing
   408→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   409→	if err != nil {
   410→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   411→	}
   412→
   413→	// Parse hook input
   414→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookPostToolUse, os.Stdin)
   415→	if err != nil {

   418→

   420→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "gemini-after-tool",

   431→
   432→// handleGeminiBeforeAgent handles the BeforeAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   433→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's UserPromptSubmit - it fires when the user submits a prompt.
   434→// We capture the initial state here so we can track what files were modified during the session.
   435→// It also checks for concurrent sessions and blocks if another session has uncommitted changes.
   436→func handleGeminiBeforeAgent() error {
   437→	// Always use the Gemini agent for Gemini hooks (don't use GetAgent() which may
   438→	// return Claude based on auto-detection in environments like VSCode)
   439→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   440→	if err != nil {
   441→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	// Parse hook input - BeforeAgent provides user prompt info similar to UserPromptSubmit
   445→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   451→
   452→	// Log with prompt if available (Gemini provides the user's prompt in BeforeAgent)
   453→	logArgs := []any{
   454→		slog.String("hook", "before-agent"),
   455→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   456→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   457→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   458→	}
   459→	if input.UserPrompt != "" {
   460→		// Truncate long prompts for logging
   461→		promptPreview := input.UserPrompt
   462→		if len(promptPreview) > 100 {
   463→			promptPreview = promptPreview[:100] + "..."
   464→		}
   465→		logArgs = append(logArgs, slog.String("prompt_preview", promptPreview))
   466→	}
   467→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-before-agent", logArgs...)
   468→
   469→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   470→		return errors.New("no session_id in input")
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	// Capture pre-prompt state with transcript position (Gemini-specific)
   474→	// This captures both untracked files and the current transcript message count
   475→	// so we can calculate token usage for just this prompt/response cycle
   476→	if err := CaptureGeminiPrePromptState(input.SessionID, input.SessionRef); err != nil {
   477→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-prompt state: %w", err)
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
   481→	strat := GetStrategy()
   482→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   483→		agentType := ag.Type()
   484→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(input.SessionID, agentType, input.SessionRef); err != nil {
   485→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   486→		}
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	return nil
   490→}
   491→
   492→// handleGeminiAfterAgent handles the AfterAgent hook for Gemini CLI.
   493→// This fires after the agent has finished processing and generated a response.
   494→// This is equivalent to Claude Code's "Stop" hook - it commits the session changes with metadata.
   495→// AfterAgent fires after EVERY user prompt/response cycle, making it the correct place
   496→// for checkpoint creation (not SessionEnd, which only fires on explicit exit).
   497→func handleGeminiAfterAgent() error {
   498→	// Skip on default branch for strategies that don't allow it
   499→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranchForStrategy(); skip {
   500→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping on branch '%s' - create a feature branch to use Entire tracking\n", branchName)
   501→		return nil
   502→	}
   503→
   504→	// Always use Gemini agent for Gemini hooks
   505→	ag, err := agent.Get("gemini")
   506→	if err != nil {
   507→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get gemini agent: %w", err)
   508→	}
   509→
   510→	// Parse hook input using HookStop - AfterAgent provides the same data as Stop
   511→	// (session_id, transcript_path) which is what we need for committing
   512→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   513→	if err != nil {
   514→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   518→	logging.Info(logCtx, "gemini-after-agent",
   519→		slog.String("hook", "after-agent"),
   520→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   521→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   522→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   523→	)
   524→
   525→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   526→	if sessionID == "" {
   527→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   528→	}
   529→
   530→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   531→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   532→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   533→	}
   534→
   535→	// Create session context and commit
   536→	ctx := &geminiSessionContext{
   537→		sessionID:      sessionID,
   538→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
   539→	}
   540→
   541→	if err := setupGeminiSessionDir(ctx); err != nil {
   542→		return err
   543→	}
   544→
   545→	if err := extractGeminiMetadata(ctx); err != nil {
   546→		return err
   547→	}
   548→
   549→	return commitGeminiSession(ctx)
   550→}
   551→

    13→// TaskCheckpoint contains the checkpoint information for a task
    14→type TaskCheckpoint struct {
    15→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    16→	ToolUseID      string `json:"tool_use_id"`
    17→	CheckpointUUID string `json:"checkpoint_uuid"`
    18→	AgentID        string `json:"agent_id,omitempty"`
    19→}
    20→
    21→// TaskMetadataDir returns the path to a task's metadata directory
    22→// within the session metadata directory.
    23→func TaskMetadataDir(sessionMetadataDir, toolUseID string) string {
    24→	return filepath.Join(sessionMetadataDir, "tasks", toolUseID)
    25→}
    26→
    27→// WriteTaskCheckpoint writes the checkpoint.json file to the task metadata directory.
    28→// Creates the directory if it doesn't exist.
    29→func WriteTaskCheckpoint(taskMetadataDir string, checkpoint *TaskCheckpoint) error {
    30→	// Create directory if it doesn't exist
    31→	if err := os.MkdirAll(taskMetadataDir, 0o750); err != nil {
    32→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create task metadata directory: %w", err)
    33→	}
    34→
    35→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint: %w", err)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	checkpointFile := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, paths.CheckpointFileName)
    41→	if err := os.WriteFile(checkpointFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
    42→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint file: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	return nil
    46→}
    47→
    48→// ReadTaskCheckpoint reads the checkpoint file from the task metadata directory.
    49→func ReadTaskCheckpoint(taskMetadataDir string) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
    50→	checkpointFile := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, paths.CheckpointFileName)
    51→
    52→	data, err := os.ReadFile(checkpointFile) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint file: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
    58→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &checkpoint); err != nil {
    59→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal checkpoint: %w", err)
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	return &checkpoint, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// WriteTaskPrompt writes the task prompt to the task metadata directory.
    66→func WriteTaskPrompt(taskMetadataDir, prompt string) error {
    67→	promptFile := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, paths.PromptFileName)
    68→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(prompt), 0o600); err != nil {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
    70→	}
    71→	return nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→// CopyAgentTranscript copies a subagent's transcript to the task metadata directory.
    75→// If the source transcript doesn't exist, this is a no-op (not an error).
    76→func CopyAgentTranscript(srcTranscript, taskMetadataDir, agentID string) error {
    77→	// Check if source exists
    78→	if _, err := os.Stat(srcTranscript); os.IsNotExist(err) {
    79→		// Source doesn't exist, nothing to copy
    80→		return nil
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	dstTranscript := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
    84→	return copyFile(srcTranscript, dstTranscript)
    85→}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/paul/src/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/filter/filter.go",
  "content": "// Package filter provides content filtering through external commands.\n// Used to redact sensitive data (like API keys, secrets) from session transcripts.\npackage filter\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/settings\"\n)\n\n// FilterTimeout is the maximum time allowed for filter command execution.\nconst FilterTimeout = 30 * time.Second\n\n// ErrFilterNotFound is returned when the filter command executable cannot be found.\nvar ErrFilterNotFound = errors.New(\"output filter command not found\")\n\n// ErrFilterFailed is returned when the filter command returns a non-zero exit code.\nvar ErrFilterFailed = errors.New(\"output filter failed\")\n\n// ErrFilterTimeout is returned when the filter command exceeds the timeout.\nvar ErrFilterTimeout = errors.New(\"output filter timed out\")\n\n// FilterContent pipes content through the configured filter command.\n// Returns original content if no filter is configured.\n// Returns error if filter is configured but fails (command not found, non-zero exit, timeout).\n//\n// The filename parameter is provided for context (e.g., logging) but is not passed\n// to the filter command. The filter receives content on stdin and writes to stdout.\nfunc FilterContent(ctx context.Context, content []byte, filename string) ([]byte, error) {\n\t_ = filename // Reserved for future use (logging, conditional filtering)\n\n\tfilterCmd := settings.GetOutputFilter()\n\tif len(filterCmd) == 0 {\n\t\t// No filter configured, return original content\n\t\treturn content, nil\n\t}\n\n\treturn runFilter(ctx, content, filterCmd)\n}\n\n// runFilter executes the filter command with the given content on stdin.\nfunc runFilter(ctx context.Context, content []byte, filterCmd []string) ([]byte, error) {\n\t// Create context with timeout\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, FilterTimeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Build the command\n\t//nolint:gosec // filterCmd is from trusted settings file\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, filterCmd[0], filterCmd[1:]...)\n\tcmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(content)\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\t// Run the command\n\terr := cmd.Run()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t// Check for timeout\n\t\tif errors.Is(ctx.Err(), context.DeadlineExceeded) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"%w after %s\", ErrFilterTimeout, FilterTimeout)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Check for command not found\n\t\tvar execErr *exec.Error\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &execErr) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"%w: %q\", ErrFilterNotFound, filterCmd[0])\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Check for exit error\n\t\tvar exitErr *exec.ExitError\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &exitErr) {\n\t\t\tstderrStr := stderr.String()\n\t\t\tif len(stderrStr) > 200 {\n\t\t\t\tstderrStr = stderrStr[:200] + \"...\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"%w (exit %d): %s\", ErrFilterFailed, exitErr.ExitCode(), stderrStr)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Unknown error\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"filter execution failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn stdout.Bytes(), nil\n}\n\n// MustFilter is like FilterContent but panics on error.\n// Use only in contexts where filter failure is truly unrecoverable.\nfunc MustFilter(ctx context.Context, content []byte, filename string) []byte {\n\tfiltered, err := FilterContent(ctx, content, filename)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tpanic(fmt.Sprintf(\"filter failed for %s: %v\", filename, err))\n\t}\n\treturn filtered\n}\n"
}

When no filter is configured (settings return nil), content passes through unchanged\n\tcontent := []byte(\"sensitive data: api_key=sk-12345\")\n\n\tresult, err := FilterContent(context.Background(), content, \"test.txt\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !bytes.Equal(result, content) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected content to pass through unchanged, got %q\", result)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_Success(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Test with cat - should pass content through unchanged\n\tcontent := []byte(\"hello world\")\n\n\tresult, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{\"cat\"})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !bytes.Equal(result, content) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected %q, got %q\", content, result)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_WithArgs(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Test with sed - should transform content\n\tcontent := []byte(\"secret_key\")\n\n\tresult, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{\"sed\", \"s/secret/REDACTED/\"})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\texpected := []byte(\"REDACTED_key\")\n\tif !bytes.Equal(result, expected) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected %q, got %q\", expected, result)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_CommandNotFound(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := []byte(\"test\")\n\n\t_, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{\"nonexistent-filter-command-xyz\"})\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected error for nonexistent command\")\n\t}\n\n\tif !errors.Is(err, ErrFilterNotFound) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected ErrFilterNotFound, got: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_NonZeroExit(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := []byte(\"test\")\n\n\t// Use sh -c \"exit 1\" to simulate a failed command\n\t_, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{\"sh\", \"-c\", \"echo error >&2; exit 1\"})\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected error for non-zero exit\")\n\t}\n\n\tif !errors.Is(err, ErrFilterFailed) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected ErrFilterFailed, got: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_Timeout(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Skip in short mode as this test takes time\n\tif testing.Short() {\n\t\tt.Skip(\"skipping timeout test in short mode\")\n\t}\n\n\tcontent := []byte(\"test\")\n\n\t// Create a context with a very short timeout\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// sleep command will exceed our timeout\n\t_, err := runFilter(ctx, content, []string{\"sleep\", \"10\"})\n\tif err == nil {\n\t\tt.Fatal(\"expected error for timeout\")\n\t}\n\n\tif !errors.Is(err, ErrFilterTimeout) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected ErrFilterTimeout, got: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_LargeContent(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Test with larger content to ensure piping works correctly\n\tcontent := bytes.Repeat([]byte(\"x\"), 1024*1024) // 1MB\n\n\tresult, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{\"cat\"})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !bytes.Equal(result, content) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"content mismatch for large input\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_EmptyContent(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := []byte{}\n\n\tresult, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{\"cat\"})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(result) != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected empty result, got %q\", result)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_MultilineContent(t *testing.T) {\n\tcontent := []byte(\"line1\\nline2\\nline3\\n\")\n\n\tresult, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{\"cat\"})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif !bytes.Equal(result, content) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected %q, got %q\", content, result)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestRunFilter_ScriptFilter(t *testing.T) {\n\t// Create a temporary script that acts as a filter\n\ttmpDir := t.TempDir()\n\tscriptPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, \"filter.sh\")\n\n\tscript := `#!/bin/sh\nsed 's/api_key=[^[:space:]]*/api_key=REDACTED/g'\n`\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(scriptPath, []byte(script), 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write script: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tcontent := []byte(\"config: api_key=sk-12345 other=value\")\n\n\tresult, err := runFilter(context.Background(), content, []string{scriptPath})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\texpected := []byte(\"config: api_key=REDACTED other=value\")\n\tif !bytes.Equal(result, expected) {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected %q, got %q\", expected, result)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to chunk transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write chunk files\n\tfor i, chunk := range chunks {\n\t\tchunkPath := basePath + err\n\t}\n\tentries[basePath+paths.ContentHashFileName] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\tName: basePath + paths.ContentHashFileName,\n\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\tHash: hashBlob,\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"

   215→
   216→// writeStandardCheckpointEntries writes session files to numbered subdirectories and
   217→// maintains a CheckpointSummary at the root level with aggregated statistics.
   218→//
   219→// Structure:
   220→//
   221→//	basePath/
   222→//	├── metadata.json         # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats)
   223→//	├── 1/                    # First session
   224→//	│   ├── metadata.json     # CommittedMetadata (session-specific, includes initial_attribution)
   225→//	│   ├── full.jsonl
   226→//	│   ├── prompt.txt
   227→//	│   ├── context.md
   228→//	│   └── content_hash.txt
   229→//	├── 2/                    # Second session
   230→//	└── ...
   231→func (s *GitStore) writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   232→	// Read existing summary to get current session count
   233→	var existingSummary *CheckpointSummary
   234→	metadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
   235→	if entry, exists := entries[metadataPath]; exists {
   236→		existing, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   237→		if err == nil {
   238→			existingSummary = existing
   239→		}
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Determine session index (0, 1, 2, ...) - 0-based numbering
   243→	sessionIndex := 0
   244→	if existingSummary != nil {
   245→		sessionIndex = len(existingSummary.Sessions)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	// Write session files to numbered subdirectory
   249→	sessionPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/", basePath, sessionIndex)
   250→	sessionFilePaths, err := s.writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts, sessionPath, entries)
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return err
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Copy additional metadata files from directory if specified (to session subdirectory)
   256→	if opts.MetadataDir != "" {
   257→		if err := s.copyMetadataDir(opts.MetadataDir, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   258→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy metadata directory: %w", err)
   259→		}
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	// Update root metadata.json with CheckpointSummary
   263→	return s.writeCheckpointSummary(opts, basePath, entries, existingSummary, sessionFilePaths)
   264→}

    34→
    35→// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/sessions branch.
    36→// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    37→//
    38→// For task checkpoints (IsTask=true), additional files are written under tasks/<tool-use-id>/:
    39→//   - For incremental checkpoints: checkpoints/NNN-<tool-use-id>.json
    40→//   - For final checkpoints: checkpoint.json and agent-<agent-id>.jsonl
    41→func (s *GitStore) WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error {
    42→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
    43→
    44→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
    45→	if opts.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    46→		return errors.New("invalid checkpoint options: checkpoint ID is required")
    47→	}
    48→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
    49→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    50→	}
    51→	if err := validation.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
    52→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	if err := validation.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    56→	}

   288→
   289→// addTaskMetadataToTree adds task checkpoint metadata to a git tree.
   290→// When IsIncremental is true, only adds the incremental checkpoint file.
   291→func (s *GitStore) addTaskMetadataToTree(baseTreeHash plumbing.Hash, opts WriteTemporaryTaskOptions) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   292→	// Get base tree and flatten it
   293→	baseTree, err := s.repo.TreeObject(baseTreeHash)
   294→	if err != nil {
   295→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get base tree: %w", err)
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   299→	if err := FlattenTree(s.repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   300→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to flatten tree: %w", err)
   301→	}
   302→
   303→	// Compute metadata paths
   304→	sessionMetadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + opts.SessionID
   305→	taskMetadataDir := sessionMetadataDir + "/tasks/" + opts.ToolUseID
   306→
   307→	if opts.IsIncremental {
   308→		// Incremental checkpoint: only add the checkpoint file
   309→		// Use proper JSON marshaling to handle nil/empty IncrementalData correctly
   310→		incrementalCheckpoint := struct {
   311→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   312→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   313→			Timestamp time.Time       `json:"timestamp"`
   314→			Data      json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
   315→		}{
   316→			Type:      opts.IncrementalType,
   317→			ToolUseID: opts.ToolUseID,
   318→			Timestamp: time.Now().UTC(),
   319→			Data:      opts.IncrementalData,
   320→		}
   321→		cpData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(incrementalCheckpoint, "", "  ")
   322→		if err != nil {
   323→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   324→		}
   325→
   326→		cpBlobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, cpData)
   327→		if err != nil {
   328→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to create incremental checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   329→		}
   330→		cpFilename := fmt.Sprintf("%03d-%s.json", opts.IncrementalSequence, opts.ToolUseID)
   331→		cpPath := taskMetadataDir + "/checkpoints/" + cpFilename
   332→		entries[cpPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   333→			Name: cpPath,
   334→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   335→			Hash: cpBlobHash,
   336→		}
   337→	} else {
   338→		// Final checkpoint: add transcripts and checkpoint.json
   339→
   340→		// Add session transcript (with chunking support for large transcripts)
   341→		if opts.TranscriptPath != "" {
   342→			if transcriptContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.TranscriptPath); readErr == nil {
   343→				// Detect agent type from content for proper chunking
   344→				agentType := agent.DetectAgentTypeFromContent(transcriptContent)
   345→
   346→				// Chunk if necessary
   347→				chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcriptContent, agentType)
   348→				if chunkErr != nil {
   349→					logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to chunk transcript, checkpoint will be saved without transcript",
   350→						slog.String("error", chunkErr.Error()),
   351→						slog.String("session_id", opts.SessionID),
   352→					)
   353→				} else {
   354→					for i, chunk := range chunks {
   355→						chunkPath := sessionMetadataDir + "/" + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)
   356→						blobHash, blobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)
   357→						if blobErr != nil {
   358→							logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to create blob for transcript chunk",
   359→								slog.String("error", blobErr.Error()),
   360→								slog.String("session_id", opts.SessionID),
   361→								slog.Int("chunk_index", i),
   362→							)
   363→							continue
   364→						}
   365→						entries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   366→							Name: chunkPath,
   367→							Mode: filemode.Regular,
   368→							Hash: blobHash,
   369→						}
   370→					}
   371→				}
   372→			}
   373→		}
   374→
   375→		// Add subagent transcript if available
   376→		if opts.SubagentTranscriptPath != "" && opts.AgentID != "" {
   377→			if agentContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.SubagentTranscriptPath); readErr == nil {
   378→				if blobHash, blobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, agentContent); blobErr == nil {
   379→					agentPath := taskMetadataDir + "/agent-" + opts.AgentID + ".jsonl"
   380→					entries[agentPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   381→						Name: agentPath,
   382→						Mode: filemode.Regular,
   383→						Hash: blobHash,
   384→					}
   385→				}
   386→			}
   387→		}
   388→
   389→		// Add checkpoint.json
   390→		checkpointJSON := fmt.Sprintf(`{
   391→  "session_id": %q,
   392→  "tool_use_id": %q,
   393→  "checkpoint_uuid": %q,
   394→  "agent_id": %q
   395→}`, opts.SessionID, opts.ToolUseID, opts.CheckpointUUID, opts.AgentID)
   396→
   397→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(checkpointJSON))
   398→		if err != nil {
   399→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to create checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   400→		}
   401→		checkpointPath := taskMetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
   402→		entries[checkpointPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   403→			Name: checkpointPath,
   404→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   405→			Hash: blobHash,
   406→		}
   407→	}
   408→
   409→	// Build new tree from entries
   410→	return BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
   411→}

   415→// The sessionID filter, if provided, limits results to commits from that session.

   417→func (s *GitStore) ListTemporaryCheckpoints(ctx context.Context, baseCommit, worktreeID, sessionID string, limit int) ([]TemporaryCheckpointInfo, error) {

err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to flatten tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Compute metadata paths\n\tsessionMetadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + \"/\" + opts.SessionID\n\ttaskMetadataDir := sessionMetadataDir + \"/tasks/\" + opts.ToolUseID\n\n\tif opts.IsIncremental {\n\t\t// Incremental checkpoint: only add the checkpoint file\n\t\t// Use proper JSON marshaling to handle nil/empty IncrementalData correctly\n\t\tincrementalCheckpoint := struct {\n\t\t\tType      string          `json:\"type\"`\n\t\t\tToolUseID string          `json:\"tool_use_id\"`\n\t\t\tTimestamp time.Time       `json:\"timestamp\"`\n\t\t\tData      json.RawMessage `json:\"data\"`\n\t\t}{\n\t\t\tType:      opts.IncrementalType,\n\t\t\tToolUseID: opts.ToolUseID,\n\t\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\t\tData:      opts.IncrementalData,\n\t\t}\n\t\tcpData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(incrementalCheckpoint, \"\", \"  \")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal incremental checkpoint: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcpBlobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, cpData)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create incremental checkpoint blob: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcpFilename := fmt.Sprintf(\"%03d-%s.json\", opts.IncrementalSequence, opts.ToolUseID)\n\t\tcpPath := taskMetadataDir + \"/checkpoints/\" + cpFilename\n\t\tentries[cpPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\tName: cpPath,\n\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\tHash: cpBlobHash,\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// Final checkpoint: add transcripts and checkpoint.json\n\n\t\t// Add session transcript (with chunking support for large transcripts)\n\t\tif opts.TranscriptPath != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif transcriptContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.TranscriptPath); readErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Detect agent type from content for proper chunking\n\t\t\t\tagentType := agent.DetectAgentTypeFromContent(transcriptContent)\n\n\t\t\t\t// Chunk if necessary\n\t\t\t\tchunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcriptContent, agentType)\n\t\t\t\tif chunkErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogging.Warn(context.Background(), \"failed to chunk transcript, checkpoint will be saved without transcript\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", chunkErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", opts.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tfor i, chunk := range chunks {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tchunkPath := sessionMetadataDir + \"/\" + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)\n\t\t\t\t\t\tblobHash, blobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif blobErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tlogging.Warn(context.Background(), \"failed to create blob for transcript chunk\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", blobErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", opts.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.Int(\"chunk_index\", i),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t\tentries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tName: chunkPath,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Add subagent transcript if available\n\t\tif opts.SubagentTranscriptPath != \"\" && opts.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif agentContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.SubagentTranscriptPath); readErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\tif blobHash, blobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, agentContent); blobErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tagentPath := taskMetadataDir + \"/agent-\" + opts.AgentID + \".jsonl\"\n\t\t\t\t\tentries[agentPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tName: agentPath,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Add checkpoint.json\n\t\tcheckpointJSON := fmt.Sprintf(`{\n  \"session_id\": %q,\n  \"tool_use_id\": %q,\n  \"checkpoint_uuid\": %q,\n  \"agent_id\": %q\n}`, opts.SessionID, opts.ToolUseID, opts.CheckpointUUID, opts.AgentID)\n\n\t\tblobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(checkpointJSON))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create checkpoint blob: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcheckpointPath := taskMetadataDir + \"/checkpoint.json\"\n\t\tentries[checkpointPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\tName: checkpointPath,\n\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Build new tree from entries\n\treturn BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n}",
  "new_string": "// addTaskMetadataToTree adds task checkpoint metadata to a git tree.\n// When IsIncremental is true, only adds the incremental checkpoint file.\n// Transcripts are filtered through the configured output filter.\nfunc (s *GitStore) addTaskMetadataToTree(ctx context.Context, baseTreeHash plumbing.Hash, opts WriteTemporaryTaskOptions) (plumbing.Hash, error) {\n\t// Get base tree and flatten it\n\tbaseTree, err := s.repo.TreeObject(baseTreeHash)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get base tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tentries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)\n\tif err := FlattenTree(s.repo, baseTree, \"\", entries); err != nil {\n\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to flatten tree: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Compute metadata paths\n\tsessionMetadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + \"/\" + opts.SessionID\n\ttaskMetadataDir := sessionMetadataDir + \"/tasks/\" + opts.ToolUseID\n\n\tif opts.IsIncremental {\n\t\t// Incremental checkpoint: only add the checkpoint file\n\t\t// Use proper JSON marshaling to handle nil/empty IncrementalData correctly\n\t\tincrementalCheckpoint := struct {\n\t\t\tType      string          `json:\"type\"`\n\t\t\tToolUseID string          `json:\"tool_use_id\"`\n\t\t\tTimestamp time.Time       `json:\"timestamp\"`\n\t\t\tData      json.RawMessage `json:\"data\"`\n\t\t}{\n\t\t\tType:      opts.IncrementalType,\n\t\t\tToolUseID: opts.ToolUseID,\n\t\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\t\tData:      opts.IncrementalData,\n\t\t}\n\t\tcpData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(incrementalCheckpoint, \"\", \"  \")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal incremental checkpoint: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tcpBlobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, cpData)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create incremental checkpoint blob: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcpFilename := fmt.Sprintf(\"%03d-%s.json\", opts.IncrementalSequence, opts.ToolUseID)\n\t\tcpPath := taskMetadataDir + \"/checkpoints/\" + cpFilename\n\t\tentries[cpPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\tName: cpPath,\n\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\tHash: cpBlobHash,\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\t// Final checkpoint: add transcripts and checkpoint.json\n\n\t\t// Add session transcript (with chunking support for large transcripts)\n\t\tif opts.TranscriptPath != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif transcriptContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.TranscriptPath); readErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Filter the transcript through configured output filter\n\t\t\t\tfilteredTranscript, filterErr := filter.FilterContent(ctx, transcriptContent, paths.TranscriptFileName)\n\t\t\t\tif filterErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to filter transcript: %w\", filterErr)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\t// Detect agent type from content for proper chunking\n\t\t\t\tagentType := agent.DetectAgentTypeFromContent(filteredTranscript)\n\n\t\t\t\t// Chunk if necessary\n\t\t\t\tchunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkTranscript(filteredTranscript, agentType)\n\t\t\t\tif chunkErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"failed to chunk transcript, checkpoint will be saved without transcript\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", chunkErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", opts.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tfor i, chunk := range chunks {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tchunkPath := sessionMetadataDir + \"/\" + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)\n\t\t\t\t\t\tblobHash, blobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif blobErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tlogging.Warn(ctx, \"failed to create blob for transcript chunk\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", blobErr.Error()),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", opts.SessionID),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tslog.Int(\"chunk_index\", i),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t\tentries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tName: chunkPath,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Add subagent transcript if available (filtered)\n\t\tif opts.SubagentTranscriptPath != \"\" && opts.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif agentContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.SubagentTranscriptPath); readErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\tagentFilename := \"agent-\" + opts.AgentID + \".jsonl\"\n\t\t\t\tfilteredContent, filterErr := filter.FilterContent(ctx, agentContent, agentFilename)\n\t\t\t\tif filterErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to filter subagent transcript: %w\", filterErr)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif blobHash, blobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, filteredContent); blobErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tagentPath := taskMetadataDir + \"/\" + agentFilename\n\t\t\t\t\tentries[agentPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tName: agentPath,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Add checkpoint.json\n\t\tcheckpointJSON := fmt.Sprintf(`{\n  \"session_id\": %q,\n  \"tool_use_id\": %q,\n  \"checkpoint_uuid\": %q,\n  \"agent_id\": %q\n}`, opts.SessionID, opts.ToolUseID, opts.CheckpointUUID, opts.AgentID)\n\n\t\tblobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(checkpointJSON))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create checkpoint blob: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tcheckpointPath := taskMetadataDir + \"/checkpoint.json\"\n\t\tentries[checkpointPath] = object.TreeEntry{\n\t\t\tName: checkpointPath,\n\t\t\tMode: filemode.Regular,\n\t\t\tHash: blobHash,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Build new tree from entries\n\treturn BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)\n}"
}

   226→// WriteTemporaryTask writes a task checkpoint to a shadow branch.
   227→// Task checkpoints include both code changes and task-specific metadata.
   228→// Returns the commit hash of the created checkpoint.
   229→func (s *GitStore) WriteTemporaryTask(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryTaskOptions) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   230→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   231→
   232→	// Validate base commit - required for shadow branch naming
   233→	if opts.BaseCommit == "" {
   234→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, errors.New("BaseCommit is required for task checkpoint")
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
   238→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
   239→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("invalid task checkpoint options: %w", err)
   240→	}
   241→	if err := validation.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   242→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("invalid task checkpoint options: %w", err)
   243→	}
   244→	if err := validation.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
   245→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("invalid task checkpoint options: %w", err)
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	// Get shadow branch name
   249→	shadowBranchName := ShadowBranchNameForCommit(opts.BaseCommit, opts.WorktreeID)
   250→
   251→	// Get or create shadow branch
   252→	parentHash, baseTreeHash, err := s.getOrCreateShadowBranch(shadowBranchName)
   253→	if err != nil {
   254→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get shadow branch: %w", err)
   255→	}
   256→
   257→	// Collect all files to include in the commit
   258→	allFiles := make([]string, 0, len(opts.ModifiedFiles)+len(opts.NewFiles))
   259→	allFiles = append(allFiles, opts.ModifiedFiles...)
   260→	allFiles = append(allFiles, opts.NewFiles...)
   261→
   262→	// Build new tree with code changes (no metadata dir yet)
   263→	newTreeHash, err := s.buildTreeWithChanges(baseTreeHash, allFiles, opts.DeletedFiles, "", "")
   264→	if err != nil {
   265→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to build tree: %w", err)
   266→	}
   267→
   268→	// Add task metadata to tree
   269→	newTreeHash, err = s.addTaskMetadataToTree(newTreeHash, opts)
   270→	if err != nil {
   271→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to add task metadata: %w", err)
   272→	}
   273→
   274→	// Create the commit
   275→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, parentHash, opts.CommitMessage, opts.AuthorName, opts.AuthorEmail)
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to create commit: %w", err)
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	// Update shadow branch reference
   281→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   282→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
   283→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   284→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to update shadow branch reference: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	return commitHash, nil
   288→}

   207→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   208→	if err != nil {

    10→// IsAccessibleMode returns true if accessibility mode should be enabled.
    11→// This checks the ACCESSIBLE environment variable.
    12→// Set ACCESSIBLE=1 (or any non-empty value) to enable accessible mode,
    13→// which uses simpler prompts that work better with screen readers.
    14→func IsAccessibleMode() bool {

    23→// NewAccessibleForm creates a new huh form with accessibility mode
    24→// enabled if the ACCESSIBLE environment variable is set.
    25→// Note: WithAccessible() is only available on forms, not individual fields.
    26→// Always wrap confirmations and other prompts in a form to enable accessibility.
    27→func NewAccessibleForm(groups ...*huh.Group) *huh.Form {
    28→	form := huh.NewForm(groups...).WithTheme(entireTheme())
    29→	if IsAccessibleMode() {

     1→package cli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// TaskCheckpoint contains the checkpoint information for a task
    14→type TaskCheckpoint struct {
    15→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    16→	ToolUseID      string `json:"tool_use_id"`
    17→	CheckpointUUID string `json:"checkpoint_uuid"`
    18→	AgentID        string `json:"agent_id,omitempty"`
    19→}
    20→
    21→// TaskMetadataDir returns the path to a task's metadata directory
    22→// within the session metadata directory.
    23→func TaskMetadataDir(sessionMetadataDir, toolUseID string) string {
    24→	return filepath.Join(sessionMetadataDir, "tasks", toolUseID)
    25→}
    26→
    27→// WriteTaskCheckpoint writes the checkpoint.json file to the task metadata directory.
    28→// Creates the directory if it doesn't exist.
    29→func WriteTaskCheckpoint(taskMetadataDir string, checkpoint *TaskCheckpoint) error {
    30→	// Create directory if it doesn't exist
    31→	if err := os.MkdirAll(taskMetadataDir, 0o750); err != nil {
    32→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create task metadata directory: %w", err)
    33→	}
    34→
    35→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint: %w", err)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	checkpointFile := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, paths.CheckpointFileName)
    41→	if err := os.WriteFile(checkpointFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
    42→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint file: %w", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	return nil
    46→}
    47→
    48→// ReadTaskCheckpoint reads the checkpoint file from the task metadata directory.
    49→func ReadTaskCheckpoint(taskMetadataDir string) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
    50→	checkpointFile := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, paths.CheckpointFileName)
    51→
    52→	data, err := os.ReadFile(checkpointFile) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint file: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
    58→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &checkpoint); err != nil {
    59→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal checkpoint: %w", err)
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	return &checkpoint, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// WriteTaskPrompt writes the task prompt to the task metadata directory.
    66→func WriteTaskPrompt(taskMetadataDir, prompt string) error {
    67→	promptFile := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, paths.PromptFileName)
    68→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(prompt), 0o600); err != nil {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
    70→	}
    71→	return nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→// CopyAgentTranscript copies a subagent's transcript to the task metadata directory.
    75→// If the source transcript doesn't exist, this is a no-op (not an error).
    76→func CopyAgentTranscript(srcTranscript, taskMetadataDir, agentID string) error {
    77→	// Check if source exists
    78→	if _, err := os.Stat(srcTranscript); os.IsNotExist(err) {
    79→		// Source doesn't exist, nothing to copy
    80→		return nil
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	dstTranscript := filepath.Join(taskMetadataDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
    84→	return copyFile(srcTranscript, dstTranscript)
    85→}

> AGENT

Committed as `6fe5d8cb`:

```
Add output_filter setting to redact secrets from session transcripts

Add a new `output_filter` setting that pipes sensitive file contents through
an external command (like gitleaks-filter) before writing to git. This allows
automatic redaction of API keys, secrets, and other sensitive data from
session transcripts stored in the entire/sessions branch.

Changes:
- Add OutputFilter []string field to EntireSettings with merge support
- Create filter package with Content() function for filtering through
  external commands (30s timeout, proper error handling)
- Integrate filtering into checkpoint/committed.go for transcripts,
  prompts, context, and subagent transcripts written to git
- Integrate filtering into checkpoint/temporary.go for shadow branch
  task checkpoints
- Filter sensitive files (full.jsonl, prompt.txt, context.md, agent-*.jsonl)
  but not structural metadata (metadata.json, checkpoint.json)

Configuration example in .entire/settings.json:
  {"output_filter": ["gitleaks-filter", "--redact"]}
```
